# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-21)

## Corpus Check
- 4732 files · ~10,976,452 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 61470 nodes · 163646 edges · 3036 communities (1435 shown, 1601 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 4570 edges (avg confidence: 0.72)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `39e53f70`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
